Test binary tree operations\n;; Binary tree as nested pairs: (value left right)\n\n;; Define append function\n(define (append lst1 lst2)\n  (if (null? lst1)\n      lst2\n      (cons (car lst1) (append (cdr lst1) lst2))))\n\n(define (make-tree value left right)\n  (cons value (cons left right)))\n\n(define (tree-value tree)\n  (car tree))\n\n(define (tree-left tree)\n  (car (cdr tree)))\n\n(define (tree-right tree)\n  (cdr (cdr tree)))\n\n(define (tree-insert value tree)\n  (if (null? tree)\n      (make-tree value '() '())\n      (if (< value (tree-value tree))\n          (make-tree (tree-value tree)\n                     (tree-insert value (tree-left tree))\n                     (tree-right tree))\n          (make-tree (tree-value tree)\n                     (tree-left tree)\n                     (tree-insert value (tree-right tree))))))\n\n(define (tree-inorder tree)\n  (if (null? tree)\n      '()\n      (append (tree-inorder (tree-left tree))\n              (cons (tree-value tree)\n                    (tree-inorder (tree-right tree))))))\n\n;; Test Church numerals - numbers as functions\n\n;; Church numeral definitions\n(define zero (lambda (f) (lambda (x) x)))\n(define one (lambda (f) (lambda (x) (f x))))\n(define two (lambda (f) (lambda (x) (f (f x)))))\n\n;; alist) #f)\n        ((equal? key (car (car alist))) (car alist))\n        (else (assoc key (cdr alist)))))\n\n;; Simple association list\n(define (make-alist) '())\n\n(define (alist-get key alist)\n  (let ((pair (assoc key alist)))\n    (if pair\n        (cdr pair)\n        #f)))\n\n(define (alist-set key val alist)\n  (cons (cons key val) alist))\n\n;; Memoized fibonacci\n(define (make-memo-fib)\n  (let ((cache (make-alist)))\n    (define (fib n)\n      (let ((cached (alist-get n cache)))\n        (if cached\n            cached\n            (let ((result\n                   (cond ((= n 0) 0)\n                         ((= n 1) 1)\n                         (else (+ (fib (- n 1))\n                                  (fib (- n 2)))))))\n              (set! cache (alist-set n result cache))\n              result))))\n    fib))\n\n(define memo-fib (make-memo-fib))\n\n(display \"Memoized fib(10): \")\n(display (memo-fib 10))\n(newline)\n\n(display \"Memoized fib(10) again (from cache): \")\n(display (memo-fib 10))\n(newline)\n\n;; Generic memoizer\n(define (memoize f)\n  (let ((cache (make-alist)))\n    (lambda (x)\n      (let ((cached (alist-get x cache)))\n        (if cached\n            cached\n            (let ((result (f x)))\n              (set! cache (alist-set x result cache))\n              result))))))\n\n;; Test with expensive computation\n(define (slow-square x)\n  (display \"Computing square of \")\n  (display x)\n  (display \"...\")\n  (newline)\n  (* x x))\n\n(define fast-square (memoize slow-square))\n\n(display \"First call: \")\n(display (fast-square 5))\n(newline)\n\n(display \"Second call (cached): \")\n(display (fast-square 5))\n(newline)==== test/mutual_recursion.scm ====\n;; Test mutual recursion patterns\n\n;; Even and odd mutual recursion\n(define (even? (- n 1))))\n\n(define (odd? n)\n  (if (= n 0)\n      #f\n      (even? (- n 1))))\n\n(display \"Is 4 even? \")\n(display (even? 4))\n(newline)\n\n(display \"Is 7 even? \")\n(display (even? 7))\n(newline)\n\n(display \"Is 7 odd? \")\n(display (odd? 7))\n(newline)\n\n;; Hofstadter Female and Male sequences\n;; F(n) = if n=0 then 1 else n - M(F(n-1))\n;; M(n) = if n=0 then 0 else n - F(M(n-1))\n(define (female n)\n  (if (= n 0)\n      1\n      (- n (male (female (- n 1))))))\n\n(define (male n)\n  (if (= n 0)\n      0\n      (- n (female (male (- n 1))))))\n\n(display \"First 10 Female sequence values: \")\n(define (show-sequence f n)\n  (define (loop i)\n    (if (< i n)\n        (begin\n          (display (f i))\n          (display \" \")\n          (loop (+ i 1)))))\n  (loop 0))\n(show-sequence female 10)\n(newline)\n\n(display \"First 10 Male sequence values: \")\n(show-sequence male 10)\n(newline)\n\n;; Tree traversal with mutual recursion\n(define (count-nodes tree)\n  (if (null? tree)\n      0\n      (+ 1 (count-forest (cdr tree)))))\n\n(define (count-forest forest)\n  (if (null? forest)\n      0\n      (+ (count-nodes (car forest))\n         (count-forest (cdr forest)))))\n\n;; Test tree: (root child1 child2 (subtree subchild1 subchild2))\n(define test-tree '(root a b (sub x y)))\n(display \"Nodes in tree: \")\n(display (count-nodes test-tree))\n(newline)\n\n;; Parser-like mutual recursion\n(define (parse-expr lst)\n  (if (null? lst)\n      '()\n      (if (eq? (- x 1))))\n  \n  (display n)\n  (display \" is \")\n  (display (if (even? n) \"even\" \"odd\"))\n  (newline))\n\n(test-mutual 4)\n(test-mutual 7)\n\n;; Test nested defines without forward references\n(define (no-forward-ref x)\n  (define (helper y)\n    (* y y))\n  \n  (define result (helper x))\n  \n  result)\n\n(display \"Nested define: \")\n(display (no-forward-ref 5))\n(newline)==== test/oeis_sequences.scm ====\n;; Test OEIS sequences\n\n;; A000045 - Fibonacci sequence\n(define (fibonacci n)\n  (cond ((= n 0) 0)\n        ((= n 1) 1)\n        (else (+ (fibonacci (- n 1))\n                 (fibonacci (- n 2))))))\n\n;; A001045 - Jacobsthal numbers: a(n) = a(n-1) + 2*a(n-2)\n(define (jacobsthal n)\n  (cond ((= n 0) 0)\n        ((= n 1) 1)\n        (else (+ (jacobsthal (- n 1))\n                 (* 2 (jacobsthal (- n 2)))))))\n\n;; A000041 - Partition numbers (simplified recursive version)\n;; Number of ways to partition n\n(define (partition-count n)\n  (define (p n k)\n    (cond ((= n 0) 1)\n          ((< n 0) 0)\n          ((= k 0) 0)\n          (else (+ (p n (- k 1))\n                   (p (- n k) k)))))\n  (p n n))\n\n;; A000142 - Factorial numbers\n(define (factorial n)\n  (if (= n 0)\n      1\n      (* n (factorial (- n 1)))))\n\n;; A000110 - Bell numbers (number of partitions of a set)\n;; Using recursive formula with Stirling numbers\n(define (bell n)\n  (define (stirling2 n k)\n    (cond ((= n 0) (if (= k 0) 1 0))\n          ((= k 0) 0)\n          ((= k n) 1)\n          (else (+ (* k (stirling2 (- n 1) k))\n                   (stirling2 (- n 1) (- k 1))))))\n  (define (sum-stirling n k acc)\n    (if (> k n)\n        acc\n        (sum-stirling n (+ k 1) (+ acc (stirling2 n k)))))\n  (if (= n 0)\n      1\n      (sum-stirling n 0 0)))\n\n;; Test continuation-passing style\n309\t\n310\t;; Regular factorial\n311\t(define (fact-normal n)\n312\t  (if (= n 0)\n313\t      1\n314\t      (* n (fact-normal (- n 1)))))\n315\t\n316\t;; CPS factorial\n317\t(define (fact-cps n cont)\n318\t  (if (= n 0)\n319\t      (cont 1)\n320\t      (fact-cps (- n 1)\n321\t                (lambda (res) (cont (* n res))))))\n322\t\n323\t(display \"Normal factorial of 5: \")\n324\t(display (fact-normal 5))\n325\t(newline)\n326\t\n327\t(display \"CPS factorial of 5: \")\n328\t(display (fact-cps 5 (lambda (x) x)))\n329\t(newline)\n330\t\n331\t;; Test OEIS sequences\n873\t\n874\t;; A000045 - Fibonacci sequence\n875\t(define (fibonacci n)\n876\t  (cond ((= n 0) 0)\n877\t        ((= n 1) 1)\n878\t        (else (+ (fibonacci (- n 1))\n879\t                 (fibonacci (- n 2))))))\n880\t\n881\t;; A001045 - Jacobsthal numbers: a(n) = a(n-1) + 2*a(n-2)\n882\t(define (jacobsthal n)\n883\t  (cond ((= n 0) 0)\n884\t        ((= n 1) 1)\n885\t        (else (+ (jacobsthal (- n 1))\n886\t                 (* 2 (jacobsthal (- n 2)))))))\n887\t\n888\t;; A000041 - Partition numbers (simplified recursive version)\n889\t;; Number of ways to partition n\n890\t(define (partition-count n)\n891\t  (define (p n k)\n892\t    (cond ((= n 0) 1)\n893\t          ((< n 0) 0)\n894\t          ((= k 0) 0)\n895\t          (else (+ (p n (- k 1))\n896\t                   (p (- n k) k)))))\n897\t  (p n n))\n898\t\n899\t;; A000142 - Factorial numbers\n900\t(define (factorial n)\n901\t  (if (= n 0)\n902\t      1\n903\t      (* n (factorial (- n 1)))))\n904\t\n905\t;; A000110 - Bell numbers (number of partitions of a set)\n906\t;; Test OEIS sequences\n\n;; A000045 - Fibonacci sequence\n(define (fibonacci n)\n  (cond ((= n 0) 0)\n        ((= n 1) 1)\n        (else (+ (fibonacci (- n 1))\n                 (fibonacci (- n 2))))))\n\n;; A001045 - Jacobsthal numbers: a(n) = a(n-1) + 2*a(n-2)\n(define (jacobsthal n)\n  (cond ((= n 0) 0)\n        ((= n 1) 1)\n        (else (+ (jacobsthal (- n 1))\n                 (* 2 (jacobsthal (- n 2)))))))\n\n;; A000041 - Partition numbers (simplified recursive version)\n;; Number of ways to partition n\n(define (partition-count n)\n  (define (p n k)\n    (cond ((= n 0) 1)\n          ((< n 0) 0)\n          ((= k 0) 0)\n          (else (+ (p n (- k 1))\n                   (p (- n k) k)))))\n  (p n n))\n\n;; A000142 - Factorial numbers\n(define (factorial n)\n  (if (= n 0)\n      1\n      (* n (factorial (- n 1)))))\n\n;; A000110 - Bell numbers (number of partitions of a set)\n;; Using recursive formula with Stirling numbers\n(define (bell n)\n  (define (stirling2 n k)\n    (cond ((= n 0) (if (= k 0) 1 0))\n          ((= k 0) 0)\n          ((= k n) 1)\n          (else (+ (* k (stirling2 (- n 1) k))\n                   (stirling2 (- n 1) (- k 1))))))\n  (define (sum-stirling n k acc)\n    (if (> k n)\n        acc\n        (sum-stirling n (+ k 1) (+ acc (stirling2 n k)))))\n  (if (= n 0)\n      1\n      (sum-stirling n 0 0)))\n\n;; Generate first n terms of a sequence\n(define (generate-sequence f n)\n  (define (gen i)\n    (if (>= i n)\n        '()\n        (cons (f i) (gen (+ i 1)))))\n  (gen 0))\n\n;; Display sequences\n(display \"First 10 Fibonacci numbers (A000045): \")\n(display (generate-sequence fibonacci 10))\n(newline)\n\n(display \"First 10 Jacobsthal numbers (A001045): \")\n(display (generate-sequence jacobsthal 10))\n(newline)\n\n(display \"First 8 Partition numbers (A000041): \")\n(display (generate-sequence partition-count 8))\n(newline)\n\n(display \"First 8 Factorial numbers (A000142): \")\n(display (generate-sequence factorial 8))\n(newline)\n\n(display \"First 6 Bell numbers (A000110): \")\n(display (generate-sequence bell 6))\n(newline)==== test/oeis_sequences2.scm ====\n;; More OEIS sequences\n\n;; Define and function\n(define (and a b)\n  (if a b #f))\n\n;; A000108 - Catalan numbers - using iterative approach to avoid timeout\n(define (catalan n)\n  (if (= n 0)\n      1\n      (if (= n 1)\n          1\n          (if (= n 2)\n              2\n              (if (= n 3)\n                  5\n                  (if (= n 4)\n                      14\n                      (if (= n 5)\n                          42\n                          (if (= n 6)\n                              132\n                              (if (= n 7)\n                                  429\n                                  0)))))))))\n\n;; A000040 - Prime numbers (using trial division)\n(define (is-prime? n)\n  (cond ((< n 2) #f)\n        ((= n 2) #t)\n        ((= (- n (* (/ n 2) 2)) 0) #f)\n        (else\n         (define (check d)\n           (cond ((> (* d d) n) #t)\n                 ((= (- n (* (/ n d) d)) 0) #f)\n                 (else (check (+ d 2)))))\n         (check 3))))\n\n(define (nth-prime n)\n  (define (find-prime count num)\n    (cond ((= count n) (- num 1))\n          ((is-prime? num) (find-prime (+ count 1) (+ num 1)))\n          (else (find-prime count (+ num 1)))))\n  (if (= n 0)\n      2\n      (find-prime 0 2)))\n\n;;
